This means, if you have a BPAC account, you do not need to enter a user name or password.</p> <p>When you click the CHIP link, you will be taken straight to the electronic record of the patient that stroke-linecap="round"></path> <path id="Vector 25" d="M13.0002 1L6.3335 7.66667" stroke="white" stroke-linecap="round"></path> </g> </svg> </span></a></p> <h4>Working from home support</h4> <p>All working from home arrangements need to be approved by your manager.</p> <h5>Keep cyber secure at home</h5> <p>If using your own computer, make sure you have up-to-date anti-virus software installed.</p> <ul> <li>Use a long password for your home router or modem such as “summertime1974!”.</li> <li>Update iPads or other mobile devices with the latest upgrades.</li> <li>Keep passwords and log-ins secure — do not share them or write them down.</li> <li>Continue to be vigilant for scam and phishing emails — do not click on suspicious links, report them using the phishing button in Outlook.</li> <li>Use multi-factor authentication for logging into regional systems via Citrix.</li> <li>Do not forward documents to your personal email or home computer.</li> </ul> <h5>First time accessing the intranet from home</h5> <p>If this is your first time logging in to your Northland DHB account using this computer, contact the helpdesk to get instructions on how to register and get your login to Citrix sent to you.</p> <ul> <li>email: <a href="&#109;&#x61;&#105;&#x6c;&#116;&#x6f;&#58;&#x68;&#101;&#x6c;&#112;&#x64;&#101;&#x73;&#107;&#x40;&#110;&#x6f;&#114;&#x74;&#104;&#x6c;&#97;&#x6e;&#100;&#x64;&#104;&#x62;&#46;&#x6f;&#114;&#x67;&#46;&#x6e;&#122;">&#104;&#x65;&#108;&#x70;&#100;&#x65;&#115;&#x6b;&#64;&#x6e;&#111;&#x72;&#116;&#x68;&#108;&#x61;&#110;&#x64;&#100;&#x68;&#98;&#x2e;&#111;&#x72;&#103;&#x2e;&#110;&#x7a;</a></li> <li>phone: <a href="tel:+64094700000">09 470 0000</a> extension 7469</li> <li>Only use the phone option if you do not have email access, as they may not be able to answer immediately.</li> </ul> <h5>Access your work email from home</h5> <p><a href="https://webmail.hanz.health.nz/owa/auth/logon.aspx?replaceCurrent=1&amp;url=http%3a%2f%2fwebmail.hanz.health.nz%2fowa%2f" class="external nhl\xxxxx "xxxxx" = your user name.</p> <h5>Improving connectivity performance</h5> <ul> <li>Use Outlook Web Access rather than using Outlook via Citrix.</li> <li>“Get on then get off” – if you have done what you need to and don’t need to be logged in, logging off will release bandwidth and licences for those performing clinical functions.</li> </ul> <h5>Help</h5> <ul> <li>Email: &#104;&#x65;&#108;&#x70;&#100;&#x65;&#115;&#x6b;&#64;&#x6e;&#111;&#x72;&#116;&#x68;&#108;&#x61;&#110;&#x64;&#100;&#x68;&#98;&#x2e;&#111;&#x72;&#103;&#x2e;&#110;&#x7a;</li> <li>Phone: <a href="tel:+64094700000">09 470 0000</a> extension 7469</li> </ul> <h5>Tips for keeping records safe</h5> <p>There are increased and additional risks to records when working from home or remotely – it is important to create and keep track of your records as usual.</p> <p><a href="https://hauoraaotearoa.sharepoint.com/sites/News/SitePages/Information-and-Records-Management-Policy.aspx?web=1" stroke-linecap="round"></path> </g> </svg> </span></a></p> <h4>Occupational health</h4> <p>We offer these services that you can book online.</p> <ul> <li>Mask fit testing booking.</li> <li>Moving and handling ka nuku nuku programme.</li> <li>Staff vaccinations.</li> <li>Pre employment clinics.</li> </ul> <h5>Mask fit testing booking</h5> <p>Mask fit testing assesses whether the wearer is obtaining a good seal between their face and the mask and minimising their risk of aerosol biohazard exposure. We are mask fit testing all patient-facing staff under the AS/NZs 1715*.</p> <p>You will be provided with details of the successfully fitted mask make and model at the time of testing.</p> <p>When you come to your appointment:</p> <ul> <li>be clean-shaven</li> <li>do not eat, drink, chew gum or smoke for at least 15 minutes before your test</li> <li>bring the clean respirator and any filters you normally use (if applicable).</li> </ul> <p>To book an appointment:</p> <ul> <li>email: <a href="&#109;&#x61;&#105;&#x6c;&#116;&#x6f;&#58;&#x6d;&#97;&#x73;&#107;&#x66;&#105;&#x74;&#64;&#x6e;&#111;&#x72;&#116;&#x68;&#108;&#x61;&#110;&#x64;&#100;&#x68;&#98;&#x2e;&#111;&#x72;&#103;&#x2e;&#110;&#x7a;">&#109;&#x61;&#115;&#x6b;&#102;&#x69;&#116;&#x40;&#110;&#x6f;&#114;&#x74;&#104;&#x6c;&#97;&#x6e;&#100;&#x64;&#104;&#x62;&#46;&#x6f;&#114;&#x67;&#46;&#x6e;&#122;</a></li> <li>phone: <a href="tel:+640212273406">021 227 3406</a></li> </ul> <h5>Moving and Handling Ka Nuku Nuku programme</h5> <p>These 90-minute practical and tailored workshops are designed for staff working with patients who require assistance with moving and transferring.</p> <p>The objective is to raise the participant’s awareness of the factors that can lead to injury for themselves and their patients and to experience and apply safe handling principles to all their moving and handling tasks.</p> <p>The recommendation is that staff from each department is trained together so that the skills acquired can be practised together following training.</p> <p>Workshop content:</p> <ul> <li>Cause and effect of injury</li> <li>LITE risk assessment and management</li> <li>Principles of safe patient handling</li> <li>How to move smart and safely – biomechanics</li> <li>Area specific safe handling techniques, including theatre specific techniques such as lateral transfers prone positioning, limb holding</li> <li>Dealing with patient falls</li> <li>Equipment use such as slide sheets, handling belts, Pat slides, hovermat, hoist use</li> <li>Stretches for injury prevention</li> </ul> <p>Book your spot: <a href="https://ndhb.psecloud.co.nz/Account/Login" class="external btn-external no-external-icon" rel="btn-external external"><span class="text-wrapper">Log in — </svg> </span> </button> </h3> <div id="accordionItem11538" class="accordion-collapse collapse"> <div class="accordion-body"> <h4>Access to email and Teams</h4> <p>The Microsoft 365 portal allows you to access:</p> <ul> <li>emails</li> <li>Teams</li> <li>web versions of Office apps like Word and Excel</li> <li>any documents you have saved anywhere in the M365 cloud.</li> </ul> <p>Go to the Microsoft 365 portal and sign in with your email and your network password — for example, &#121;&#x6f;&#117;&#x72;&#46;&#x6e;&#97;&#x6d;&#101;&#x40;&#115;&#x6f;&#117;&#x74;&#104;&#x65;&#114;&#x6e;&#100;&#x68;&#98;&#x2e;&#103;&#x6f;&#118;&#x74;&#46;&#x6e;&#122;.</p> <p>You will need to have the Microsoft Authenticator app set up on a smart phone or mobile device. This is dual-factor authentication for security when accessing any Microsoft 365 tool remotely.</p> <p><a href="https://portal.office.com/" class="external btn-external no-external-icon"
